# Arena reference catalog

Generated from source directory and manifest reads. Game year means the title year, not a claimed release date. Legacy is the NHL 16-era source (2016); Recompiled retains that provenance without inventing a release year.

**These are venue asset groups, not a verified count of unique physical arenas.** Database aliases, shared environments, spare assets, and historical versions are retained. Frostbite directory suffixes are not treated as database keys. Every group links its complete observed logical filename list; each source assets.jsonl carries physical archive/CAS locations and occurrences. Payload decoding and runtime qualification are separate.
